We study sequential adversarially robust decision-aware experimental design, where data acquisition has to take into account information gain against plausible worst-case unexpected effects, modeled here as variation in adversarial variables. Building on Bayesian decision theory, we formalize an adversarially robust optimal decision under this setting and derive a principled Bayesian experimental design criterion. The criterion explicitly targets decision stability rather than nominal optimality. Experiments on synthetic and real-world scientific datasets show that conventional decision-aware design can converge rapidly to high confidence yet fragile decisions, while our robustness-aware approach yields decisions that are significantly more stable and reliable under adversarial variation.

</details>


### [137] [Federated Deep Learning for Privacy-Preserving Cardiovascular Disease Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08595)
*Hyunho Mo,Djura Smits,Mahlet A. Birhanu,Maarten J. G. Leening,Daniel Bos,Pim van der Harst,Esther E. Bron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联邦深度学习方法，用于跨异质性队列的隐私保护心血管疾病风险预测。通过整合Lifelines（148,230人，自我报告结局）和Rotterdam Study（10,155人，临床链接结局）两个不同特征的队列，联邦学习训练的深度生存模型在Rotterdam Study上C统计量从0.728提升至0.739，在Lifelines上从0.783提升至0.787，表明联邦学习能在保护患者数据隐私的同时提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病风险预测模型多依赖单中心或集中式数据，但跨机构扩展受限于隐私法规和患者级数据共享约束。联邦学习允许多中心协作建模而不传输敏感数据，然而其在医疗领域的应用面临数据集在规模、人群特征和结局定义上的异质性挑战。

Method: 采用联邦深度学习方法，整合两个异质性队列（Lifelines大型队列+Rotterdam Study小型队列），训练深度生存模型。联邦学习过程中各机构本地保留数据，仅共享模型参数梯度，实现隐私保护下的协作学习。

Result: 联邦深度生存模型在Rotterdam Study上C统计量从0.728提升至0.739（95% CI: 0.728-0.749），在Lifelines上从0.783提升至0.787（95% CI: 0.780-0.792），均优于本地训练模型。

Conclusion: 跨异质性队列的联邦深度学习能够改善心血管疾病风险预测，同时保护患者个体级数据隐私。

Abstract: Cardiovascular disease risk prediction models often rely on data from a single institution or centrally pooled datasets. Extending these models across institutions could be limited by privacy regulations and constraints on sharing patient-level data. Federated learning enables collaborative model development without transferring sensitive patient data, but its application in healthcare remains challenging because datasets often differ in size, population characteristics, and outcome definitions. In this study, we present a federated deep learning approach for privacy-preserving cardiovascular disease risk prediction that integrates two population-based cohorts with different characteristics: Lifelines, including 148,230 participants meeting the study inclusion criteria with self-reported outcomes, and the Rotterdam Study, including a smaller cohort of 10,155 participants with digitally linked clinical outcomes. Model performance was primarily evaluated on the Rotterdam Study because of its complete follow-up. Deep survival models trained using federated learning achieved higher predictive performance than models trained locally without federation. For the Rotterdam Study, the C-statistic increased from 0.728 (95% CI: 0.717-0.739) to 0.739 (95% CI: 0.728-0.749). For Lifelines, the C-statistic increased from 0.783 (95% CI: 0.775-0.791) to 0.787 (95% CI: 0.780-0.792). These findings suggest that federated deep learning across heterogeneous cohorts can improve cardiovascular disease risk prediction while preserving the privacy of individual-level patient data.

</details>


### [138] [Steering Neural Network Training through Interpretable Constraints Based on Partial Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08641)
*Yann Claes,Pierre Geurts,Vân Anh Huynh-Thu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于部分依赖的神经网络引导方法，通过将模型对特定特征的平均响应与用户提供的领域知识对齐，实现对回归问题（包括动力系统预测）的解释引导学习。实验表明，该方法相比无约束模型性能更好、数据效率更高，且生成的解释与先验知识一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性研究大多关注如何解释模型，但少有工作评估解释质量，更少有人研究如何调整模型使其解释与先验知识一致（即解释引导学习）。此外，现有方法多聚焦分类问题，且通常假设已知哪些输入特征最重要。本文旨在解决回归问题中缺乏有效的解释引导学习方法的问题。

Method: 提出基于部分依赖（partial dependence）的模型引导方法，通过约束神经网络对特定特征的平均响应，使其符合用户提供的特定函数形式的领域知识。将这种先验知识以正则化项的形式融入训练过程，从而引导模型的学习方向。

Result: 在一系列回归问题（包括动力系统预测）上的实验表明：(1) 经该方法训练的模型比无约束模型性能更好；(2) 数据效率更高；(3) 模型产生的解释确实与用户提供的先验知识对齐，而未经约束的模型则不能。

Conclusion: 基于部分依赖的解释引导学习方法可有效将领域知识融入神经网络训练，提升回归任务的性能和数据效率，并确保模型的解释与先验知识一致，弥补了现有解释引导学习主要聚焦分类问题的空白。

Abstract: Over the last few years, there has been an increased interest in making machine learning models more interpretable. Although a great deal of effort goes into developing techniques for interpreting the interactions learned by a given model, fewer studies focus on assessing the quality of such explanations. Even fewer focus on how to adjust the model to produce explanations faithful to prior knowledge, a process known as explanation-guided learning. Furthermore, most approaches in this area focus on classification problems and usually assume prior knowledge about which input features or regions are most important. In this work, we introduce a new approach to steering neural networks based on partial dependence, such that their average response to certain features aligns with specific functional domain knowledge about the problem. We empirically demonstrate on a range of regression problems, including dynamical systems forecasting, that models whose training has been controlled using our method perform better than unconstrained models and are more data-efficient. Moreover, we highlight that interpretations obtained from the former actually align with the user-provided knowledge, whereas those obtained from the latter do not.

</details>


### [139] [BiSCo-LLM: Lookup-Free Binary Spherical Coding for Extreme Low-Bit Large Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08643)
*Yuantian Shao,Peisong Wang,Zhilei Liu,Chuangyi Li,Yuanteng Chen,Pengcheng Xie,Yiwu Yao,Zhihui Wei,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BiSCo-LLM提出了一种无码本的二元球面编码框架，用于大语言模型的极端低位权重复现，通过球面二值化、残差编码和类别级恢复蒸馏实现高效压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低比特压缩方法（标量/分组量化和向量量化）在逼近2比特/权重时存在表示能力有限或需要显式码本/索引查找等额外开销的问题。

Method: 三组件流水线：(1) 局部权重块映射到单位超球面并二值化为球面码；(2) 残差BSQ阶段编码重建误差；(3) 类别级恢复蒸馏减少局部重建与模型行为的差异。另设8位保护通道用于敏感通道稳定。

Result: 报道的存储预算包含二元码、神经解码器、保护通道载荷、LoRA适配器和元数据，实现了无码本的极端低位压缩。

Conclusion: BiSCo-LLM通过无码本二元球面编码框架，在无需显式码本存储的情况下实现了极端低位权重复现，为LLM部署中的内存和带宽约束提供了新方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly constrained by memory capacity, weight bandwidth, and checkpoint storage during deployment. Existing low-bit compression methods mainly follow two directions. Scalar or group-wise quantization is simple and compatible with efficient low-precision kernels, but its representation capacity becomes limited when the target budget approaches 2 bits per weight. Vector-quantized weight compression provides a richer block-level representation, but usually introduces explicit codebooks, index lookup, and additional storage accounting. This paper presents BiSCo-LLM, a codebook-free binary spherical coding framework for extreme low-bit LLM weight compression. The core pipeline is built on three components. First, local weight chunks are mapped onto a unit hypersphere and binarized into compact spherical codes, so that the main payload is a bit-packed sign stream rather than explicit VQ centroids. Second, a residual BSQ stage encodes the reconstruction error left by the base spherical codec, providing an explicit rate-distortion path without stored codebooks. Third, category-wise recovery distillation is performed after replacing each Transformer module category, reducing the mismatch between local weight reconstruction and assembled model behavior. A small 8-bit protected-channel path is used as an auxiliary stabilization mechanism for sensitive channels and is counted separately from the BSQ payload. The reported storage budget includes binary codes, neural decoders, protected-channel payloads, LoRA adapters, and metadata.

</details>


### [140] [Multi-Modal, Multi-Environment Machine Teaching for Robust Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08647)
*Ali Larian,Qian Lin,Chang Zong Wu,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了逆强化学习中，如何通过跨多环境的异质性反馈模态来学习鲁棒的奖励函数，并提出了一种层次化机器教学算法，能有效选择环境并策略性地查询低成本反馈，从而提升奖励的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆强化学习的最优教学方法局限于单一环境且仅使用示范反馈，导致学到的奖励函数在跨环境部署时泛化能力差。需要探究多环境、多模态反馈如何共同约束奖励函数，以实现环境鲁棒的奖励学习。

Method: 首先理论分析不同反馈模态（如示范、比较）对奖励的约束能力，证明在无限数据下比较模态提供更强的全局约束。然后提出层次化机器教学算法：先贪婪选择能暴露互补奖励约束的信息性环境，再在该环境中策略性地查询低成本反馈。

Result: 在相同反馈预算下，所提方法相比均匀教学基线实现了显著更低的遗憾值和更强的跨环境泛化能力。

Conclusion: 多环境、多模态的教学策略对于学习动态鲁棒的奖励函数至关重要，为跨环境部署的可泛化奖励学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: As autonomous agents are increasingly deployed across diverse operational contexts, aligning their behavior with human intent demands reward functions that remain robust to such changes rather than overfitting to any single environment. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a principled way to infer such objectives from human feedback. However, existing analyses of optimal teaching approaches for IRL focus on single-environment, demonstration-only settings, leaving underexplored how heterogeneous feedback modalities and environment dynamics jointly constrain reward functions that generalize across multiple environments. Because demonstrations in one MDP entangle reward information with that environments specific structure, the resulting rewards frequently fail to generalize when the agent is deployed in a new setting. We first analyze how different feedback modalities constrain rewards, showing that, in the unlimited-data regime, comparisons impose strictly stronger global constraints than other modalities. Beyond this theoretical analysis, we introduce a hierarchical machine teaching algorithm for reward learning that operates across multiple MDPs. The algorithm first greedily selects informative environments that expose complementary reward constraints, then strategically queries low-cost feedback within those environments. Empirically, our method achieves substantially lower regret and stronger generalization to held-out environments than uniform teaching baselines under identical feedback budgets, demonstrating the importance of multi-environment, multi-modal teaching for learning dynamics-robust reward functions.

</details>


### [141] [Secure Decentralized Federated Learning via Gossip and Virtual Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08651)
*Amirhossein Taherpour,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出gspDAG-FL，一种去中心化联邦学习框架，通过从模型传播的gossip历史中直接推导共识，利用拓扑有向无环图（DAG）和Hashgraph风格虚拟投票实现最终性，无需全局协调开销，同时通过多重验证机制抵御拜占庭和懒惰攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化联邦学习（DFL）的gossip方法缺乏最终性和对拜占庭/懒惰参与者的鲁棒性；而账本辅助的FL虽可审计，但区块链、分片或结算委员会会引入全局协调成本，与DFL的局部性相矛盾。

Method: 节点仅与邻居交换模型负载，全节点收集事件证书和接收者认可的接受gossip证明，重构紧凑的拓扑DAG，运行Hashgraph风格虚拟投票后生成紧凑全节点证书。最终性基于唯一的模型-来源元组而非相同局部参数状态。结合负载验证、接受证明验证和私有语义审计进行聚合。

Result: 在MNIST分类和Penn Treebank语言建模任务上，网络规模达N=100，gspDAG-FL实现了接近基于验证的账本FL的学习质量，同时减少了协调瓶颈、提高了吞吐量，并在混合拜占庭和懒惰参与下保持了高无效来源检测率。

Conclusion: gspDAG-FL形式化定义了对抗性设置，证明了控制平面的安全性和条件活跃性，并在时变有效混合下给出了认证扰动gossip的收敛保证，为去中心化联邦学习提供了一种可审计、可扩展且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (DFL) removes the central server by letting nodes exchange model updates through peer-to-peer gossip, but existing gossip-based methods often lack provenance finality and resilience to Byzantine or lazy participants. Ledger-assisted federated learning (FL) improves auditability, yet blockchains, shards, or settlement committees can reintroduce global coordination costs that conflict with DFL locality. This paper proposes \emph{gspDAG-FL}, a secure DFL framework that derives consensus from the same gossip history used to disseminate models. Nodes exchange model payloads only with neighbors, while full nodes collect event certificates and receiver-endorsed accepted gossip proofs, reconstruct a compact Topology directed acyclic graph (DAG), and run Hashgraph-style virtual voting followed by compact full-node certificates. Finality is over unique model-origin tuples, not identical local parameter states. To improve resilience, gspDAG-FL combines payload validation, accepted-proof validation, and private semantic audit before aggregation. We formalize the adversarial setting, prove safety and conditional liveness of the control plane, and give a convergence guarantee for certified perturbed gossip under time-varying effective mixing. Experiments on MNIST classification and Penn Treebank language modeling, using fair held-out validation/audit data and networks up to \(N=100\), show that gspDAG-FL achieves learning quality close to validation-based ledger FL while reducing coordination bottlenecks, improving throughput, and maintaining high invalid-origin detection under mixed Byzantine and lazy participation.

</details>


### [142] [EdgeRefine: Privacy-Utility Balance for Graphs via Jaccard Sampling under Edge Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08659)
*Wenxiu Ding,Muzhi Liu,Zheng Yan,Mingjun Wang,Yifan Zhao,Qiao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EdgeRefine框架，通过自适应边细化（基于Jaccard相似度估计边概率、按隐私预算ε确定真假边比例、用采样率k控制总边数）来改善图神经网络中边级本地差分隐私的隐私-效用权衡，在多种任务和架构上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在隐私敏感场景中应用受限，因为图结构会泄露敏感链接信息。现有边级差分隐私方法通过向邻接矩阵所有元素注入噪声来模糊单条边是否存在，但强隐私需要更多噪声，过度噪声降低效用，隐私-效用平衡成为实际部署的主要障碍。

Method: EdgeRefine框架包含三个步骤：(1) 使用Jaccard相似度估计边存在概率并对边进行排序；(2) 利用隐私预算ε确定真边与假边的比例，基于概率排序分别采样；(3) 用独立采样率k控制最终图的边总数，确保图的稀疏性和可靠性。

Result: 在ε=2.5时，节点分类任务中EdgeRefine在ACM（GAT）上提升17.8%，在Cora（GCN）上提升19.7%；图分类任务中相比无噪声基线平均准确率下降仅约5%；图重构攻击下所有隐私预算内相对绝对误差均值在Cora上为1.962、AMAP上为1.472，表现出强抗泄露能力。

Conclusion: EdgeRefine通过自适应边细化机制在强隐私保护下仍能实现与无噪声基线相当的准确率，显著优于其他隐私保护方法，并有效抵御图重构攻击，为隐私保护图学习提供了实用的隐私-效用平衡方案。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have shown considerable success in learning from graph-structured data, but their use in privacy-sensitive areas remains difficult because graph structure can leak sensitive link information. To satisfy edge-level differential privacy, a common approach is to inject noise into all elements of the graph's adjacency matrix, thereby obfuscating the existence of any single edge. However, stronger privacy requires more noise, and excessive noise reduces utility, making the privacy-utility balance a major barrier to practical privacy-preserving graph learning.
  To address this issue, we propose EdgeRefine, a local differential privacy framework that improves this trade-off through adaptive edge refinement. EdgeRefine first estimates edge-existence probabilities using Jaccard similarity and ranks edges for noisy edge removal. To ensure the sparsity and reliability of the final graph, it uses the privacy budget $ε$ to determine the ratio of true to false edges, samples them separately based on this probability ranking, and controls the total number of edges with a separate sampling rate $k$. Extensive experiments show that EdgeRefine achieves accuracy comparable to the noise-free baseline and substantially outperforms other privacy-preserving methods across datasets and GNN architectures. Under privacy budget $ε= 2.5$, EdgeRefine improves node classification accuracy over state-of-the-art baselines by 17.8\% on ACM under GAT and 19.7\% on Cora under GCN. In graph classification, it achieves an average accuracy degradation of around 5\% compared to the noise-free baseline. Under graph reconstruction attacks, EdgeRefine maintains relative absolute error levels above 1 across all privacy budgets, averaging 1.962 on Cora and 1.472 on AMAP, indicating strong resilience against privacy leakage.

</details>


### [143] [Resample or Reroute? Budget-Aware Test-Time Model Selection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08665)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预算感知的测试时模型选择策略（RoR），在单次查询预算下动态分配资源用于重采样（保留原模型）或重路由（切换模型），以最大化期望正确性。基于11个开源模型在4个基准上的实验表明，该策略在成本-质量帕累托前沿上优于多种基线方法，增益大小受验证器质量影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由策略存在两个问题：一是单次提交的路由器无法达到每实例最优oracle的性能；二是重采样（resampling）虽然能恢复oracle的选型空间，但其理论保证依赖于完美的正确性标签和无限制预算，实际部署系统无法满足。此外，尚无工作将"对已提交模型重采样"和"切换路由到其他模型"视为对同一成本预算的竞争性使用。

Method: 形式化定义预算感知的测试时模型选择问题：给定每查询预算和 imperfect verifier，在每个单位预算上决定是重采样（保留当前模型）还是重路由（切换到其他模型），以最大化期望正确性。提出在线Resample-or-Route (RoR) 分配策略，基于每单位成本估计的边际正确性驱动决策，并利用选择与采样之间的可恢复性不对称性来理论化策略行为。在11个开源模型的池子上重新生成多轮正确性张量，在4个难度不同的基准上进行了回放实验。

Result: RoR策略在成本-质量帕累托前沿上优于单路由、单提交路由器、预算感知的Best-of-K、级联和随机分配等基线方法，在异构性最强的基准上增益最大。消融实验显示增益受验证器质量门控，验证器质量下降时增益缩小；在提供商标价向量和无标签一致性验证器的鲁棒性回放下，结论仍可迁移。

Conclusion: 将重采样和重路由统一纳入预算约束框架是提升LLM路由成本效益的有效途径，但实际收益依赖于验证器的质量。

Abstract: Routing among large language models (LLMs) trades response quality against serving cost, motivated by the reported gap between deployed routers and a per-instance oracle. Recent analysis shows that test-time resampling can recover per-instance selection headroom that no single-commit router captures; however, that guarantee holds only under an idealized oracle equipped with correctness labels and an unconstrained budget, neither of which a deployed system has. To the best of our knowledge, no previous work treats resampling the committed model and rerouting to an alternative model as competing uses of a single per-query cost budget. Therefore, this work formulates budget-aware test-time model selection: given a per-query budget and an imperfect verifier, allocate each unit of budget between resampling and rerouting so that expected correctness is maximized. An online resample-or-reroute (RoR) allocation policy driven by estimated marginal correctness per unit cost is proposed, and its behavior is grounded in the recoverability asymmetry between selection and sampling. Replay experiments on newly regenerated multi-draw correctness tensors from an eleven-model open-weight pool over four benchmarks of differing difficulty show that the proposed RoR policy attains a favorable cost-quality Pareto front relative to single-route, one-commit-router, budget-aware best-of-K, cascade, and random-allocation baselines for the tested pools, with the largest gains on the most heterogeneous benchmark; an ablation further shows the gains are verifier-gated, shrinking as verifier quality degrades, and robustness replays under a provider price vector and a label-free agreement verifier delineate where the conclusions carry over.

</details>


### [144] [A Practical Investigation of Training-free Relaxed Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08690)
*Guoxuan Xia,Luka Ribar,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了无训练开销的宽松推测解码技术，将其统一在共享框架下进行基准测试，发现宽松策略需要大量能力评估，且许多方法依赖质量好的草稿模型，不适合轻量级专用多令牌预测草稿器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明放宽推测解码的保真度约束（即允许偏离LLM原始采样分布）可以进一步加速、实现能力-速度权衡甚至能力提升，但缺乏系统性比较和实践指导。

Method: 将现有无训练宽松推测解码方法统一到共享框架中，在当代设置下进行基准测试，提炼经验发现。

Result: 宽松策略需要大量的能力评估（与无损推测解码不同）；许多宽松方法依赖草稿模型本身是好的语言模型，不适合轻量级专用多令牌预测草稿器。

Conclusion: 为从业者提供了实践指南和关键发现，指出了宽松推测解码的适用边界和限制条件。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates sampling from an autoregressive LLM by using a faster auxiliary model to draft tokens which are then verified in parallel by the LLM. Standard speculative decoding is lossless: its rejection and resampling steps exactly preserve the LLM's sampling distribution. Recent work argues that relaxing this strict guarantee can yield further speed-ups, controlled capability-speed trade-offs, or even capability gains. We practically investigate training-free relaxed speculative decoding techniques, unify existing approaches within a shared framework, benchmark them on contemporary settings, and distil takeaways and empirical findings for practitioners. Important takeaways include: relaxation can require considerable capability evaluation unlike lossless speculative decoding, and many relaxed approaches rely on a drafter that is a good language model, making them unsuited for lightweight dedicated multi-token-prediction drafters.

</details>


### [145] [MPFlow: Learning Budgeted Max-Flow Optimization on the Lightning Network with Deep Graph Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08703)
*Harrison Rush,Vincent Davis,Simone Antonelli,Vikash Singh,Jesse Shrader,Emanuele Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究比特币闪电网络中流动性放置问题：给定固定预算，节点应开通哪些通道以最大化路由容量。将其建模为图上预算约束的组合优化问题，选择k条边添加以最大化s-t最大流（路由容量的理论基础度量），并用图强化学习求解。轻量级agent结合消息传递策略网络与PPO及动作掩码，在枢纽排除课程下训练。在真实闪电网络快照上的实验一致优于强启发式基线。该agent已部署于生产环境，执行4640次通道开通决策，累计分配267.3 BTC（超1600万美元）于30个管理节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闪电网络中流动性放置问题：给定固定预算，节点应选择开通哪些通道以最大化其路由转发容量。

Method: 将问题形式化为图上预算约束的组合优化（选择k条边添加最大化s-t最大流），采用图强化学习方法：结合消息传递策略网络与PPO及动作掩码，并在枢纽排除课程（hub-exclusion curriculum）下训练，避免学习简单的枢纽连接策略而促使学习容量感知的放置策略。

Result: 在真实闪电网络快照上，该方法在最大流目标下一致优于强启发式基线，且已部署生产环境，执行4640次通道开通决策，累计分配267.3 BTC（超1600万美元）于30个管理节点。

Conclusion: 所提出的图强化学习方法能有效解决闪电网络中的流动性放置问题，在理论和实践上都取得了显著成果，且已实际部署应用。

Abstract: We address liquidity placement in the Bitcoin Lightning Network (LN): given a fixed budget, which channels should a node open to maximize its routing capacity? We cast this as a budget-constrained combinatorial optimization problem on graphs, selecting $k$ edge additions that maximize $s$--$t$ max-flow, a theory-grounded measure of routing capacity, and solve it with graph reinforcement learning. Our lightweight agent combines a message-passing policy network with proximal policy optimization (PPO) and action masking, and is trained under a hub-exclusion curriculum: the network's top hubs are removed from training subgraphs, forcing the policy to learn capacity-aware placement rather than hub attachment. In extensive experiments on real Lightning Network snapshots, our method consistently outperforms strong heuristic baselines on the max-flow objective across multiple seeds and unseen graphs. The agent has been deployed in production for peer recommendations, executing 4640 channel-open decisions that cumulatively allocate 267.3 BTC over $16 million across 30 managed nodes.

</details>


### [146] [Dimensionality Reduction Meets Network Science: Sensemaking on UMAP's kNN Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08746)
*Duen Horng Chau,Donghao Ren,Fred Hohman,Dominik Moritz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UMAP内部构建的kNN图蕴含着尚未被充分利用的潜力。通过在该图上应用PageRank、k-core分解和聚类系数等标准图算法，可以有效增强高维数据的可理解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型UMAP工作流仅关注其低维嵌入结果，忽视了内部kNN图所编码的高维数据流形信息，这些信息在2D投影过程中会受到扭曲。

Method: 利用UMAP内部构建的kNN图，应用三种标准图算法：(1) PageRank识别代表性数据点；(2) k-core分解揭示密集核心与稀疏边缘区域；(3) 聚类系数检测紧密相连的高度相似数据点邻域。

Result: 在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上的定性与定量评估表明，这些基于图的分析方法不仅实用，而且与专门方法（如k-medoids、HDBSCAN）相比具有竞争力或互补性。

Conclusion: UMAP内部kNN图蕴含着未被充分利用的潜力，标准图算法可有效增强高维数据可理解性，且与现有专门方法相比具有竞争力或互补性。

Abstract: While UMAP is widely used for exploring high-dimensional data, typical workflows focus on its lower-dimensional embedding, largely overlooking the rich k-nearest-neighbor (kNN) graph that UMAP constructs internally. This graph encodes the data manifold in its original high-dimensional space, before the distortion that UMAP's 2D projection introduces. We demonstrate the untapped potential of this internal representation, showing how standard graph algorithms applied to this graph enhance data sensemaking: (1) PageRank identifies representative data points, (2) k-core decomposition reveals dense core regions versus sparse periphery, and (3) clustering coefficient detects tight-knit neighborhoods with highly-similar data points. Through quantitative and qualitative evaluation on MNIST and Fashion MNIST, we show that these graph-based analyses are not only practical but also competitive with or complementary to purpose-built methods (e.g., k-medoids for exemplar selection, HDBSCAN for density-based clustering).

</details>


### [147] [Deep Learning for Joint Narrowband Interference Cancellation and Soft Demodulation in OFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08717)
*Emmanouil Kavvousanos,Francky Catthoor,Vassilis Paliouras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一深度学习框架，包含NBI-CNet和LLR-CNet两个模块，分别用于窄带干扰消除和鲁棒软解调，有效解决OFDM系统中传统方法的误码平层和LLR不可靠问题，在严重干扰下仅需0.2-0.5 dB SNR裕度即可达到最优迭代基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统压缩感知NBI消除方法具有高顺序延迟，且残留干扰为非高斯结构化的，导致基于高斯解映射器的LLR不可靠、译码器饱和及严重误码平层，亟需解决这一流水线失配问题。

Method: 提出了联合深度学习框架：(1) NBI-CNet：基于物理信息的卷积架构，单次前向传播估计并消除多音干扰，无需先验干扰源计数，计算复杂度较EOMP-IDS降低60%；(2) LLR-CNet：作为结构化白化器，将非高斯后消除残差映射为良好校准的软度量。

Result: 严重干扰(SIR=-10 dB)下，在目标BLER=10^{-4}时与最优迭代基线仅差0.2-0.5 dB SNR裕度；轻度干扰(SIR=10 dB, Q=12)下避免信号峰值混淆，获得超过3 dB编码增益；消除由干扰估计误差触发的2×10^{-4}误码平层；尺度不变设计支持任意FFT大小无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 联合深度学习框架彻底消除了传统基线在不同干扰条件下的误码平层，计算效率高且泛化能力强，为OFDM系统窄带干扰抑制提供了有效的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: Narrowband interference (NBI) severely degrades orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) systems by corrupting subcarriers and rendering classical soft demodulation ineffective. Conventional compressed-sensing (CS) mitigation exhibits high sequential latency and leaves structured, non-Gaussian residuals that cause log-likelihood ratio (LLR) unreliability, decoder saturation, and severe error floors when employing classical Gaussian demappers. We resolve this pipeline mismatch using a unified deep learning framework for joint NBI cancellation and robust soft demodulation. First, NBI-CNet employs a physics-informed convolutional architecture to estimate NBI parameters and remove multi-tone interference in a single forward pass. Without requiring prior knowledge of the active interferer count, NBI-CNet reduces computational complexity by up to 60% ($N{=}2048, Q{=}64$) compared to the state-of-the-art EOMP-IDS algorithm. Second, LLR-CNet acts as a structural whitener by mapping non-Gaussian post-mitigation residuals onto well-calibrated soft metrics. Simulations demonstrate that this joint framework eliminates the error floors inherent to traditional baselines across dense grids. Under severe interference ($\text{SIR}{=}{-}10$ dB), the pipeline operates within a $0.2$ to $0.5$ dB SNR margin of the optimal iterative baseline at a target block error rate (BLER) of $10^{-4}$. Under mild interference ($\text{SIR}{=}10$ dB) with heavy spectral overlap ($Q{=}12$), where classical greedy algorithms erroneously subtract valid data components and corrupt the payload, NBI-CNet avoids signal-peak confusion to deliver a coding gain exceeding $3$ dB. Finally, the architecture circumvents the $2{\times}10^{-4}$ error floor triggered by interferer-estimation errors, while its scale-invariant design enables robust generalization across arbitrary FFT sizes without retraining.

</details>


### [148] [SLORR: Simple and Efficient In-Training Low-Rank Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08754)
*David González-Martínez,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLORR是一种简单、无状态、保持架构不变的低秩正则化框架，基于Hoyer稀疏度量和核范数，通过GPU友好的近似方法直接在原始权重矩阵上施加正则化，在ImageNet分类和大语言模型预训练中均能以极小开销提升模型可压缩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练时低秩正则化方法存在三个主要局限：需要对大型权重矩阵进行SVD分解、修改模型架构（引入额外可训练参数）、或依赖有状态缓存量。本文旨在解决这些问题，提出一种无需SVD、不改变架构、无状态的低秩正则化方法。

Method: 提出SLORR框架，包含两个变体：基于Hoyer稀疏度量和基于核范数。直接对原始权重矩阵进行正则化，设计GPU友好的前向和反向传播近似算法，并提供近似保证。在训练过程中施加正则化以诱导低秩结构。

Result: ImageNet-1K上：ResNet-50、ViT-B/16、ViT-L/16的短周期继续训练及ResNet-18预训练中，SLORR成功诱导可压缩性，训练开销小于8%。LLM预训练（135M和560M规模）：SLORR-Hoyer训练的压缩模型性能显著优于未正则化模型，平均训练开销小于1%。

Conclusion: SLORR在多种视觉模型和大语言模型上均能以极小训练开销有效提升模型压缩后的性能保持，是一种实用且高效的低秩正则化方法。

Abstract: Low-rank factorization is widely used to compress neural networks, but modern models are often not naturally amenable to aggressive factorization without significant accuracy loss. Existing training-time low-rank regularizers can improve compressibility, but they often require SVDs of large weight matrices, modify the model architecture (introducing additional trainable parameters), or rely on stateful cached quantities. To address these limitations, we introduce SLORR, a simple, stateless, and architecture-preserving framework for in-training low-rank regularization, instantiated with two main variants based on the Hoyer sparsity metric and the nuclear norm. SLORR directly regularizes the original weight matrices using GPU-friendly approximations for the forward and backward passes of the regularizers, for which we provide approximation guarantees. We first evaluate SLORR on ImageNet-1K across short-horizon continued training of ResNet-50, ViT-B/16, and ViT-L/16, and pretraining of ResNet-18, where SLORR induces compressibility while introducing less than 8% training overhead. We further evaluate SLORR-Hoyer in LLM pretraining at 135M and 560M scales: SLORR-trained compressed models preserve performance substantially better than unregularized models while adding less than 1% average training overhead.

</details>


### [149] [Latent Memory Palace: Reasoning for Control as Autoregressive Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08724)
*Chuning Zhu,Eva Xu,Jose Barreiros,Krishnan Srinivasan,Paarth Shah,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Memory Palace (LMP)方法，通过自回归潜空间中的迭代检索实现连续控制策略的推理，将其建模为变分推断问题，并推导出潜空间强化学习技术来优化变分下界。LMP-π在仿真和真实世界任务中表现出色，具有可解释的自适应测试时计算分配能力；LMP-tok作为变长动作分词器，显著提升了自回归策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类决策具有高度灵活性——有些行动立即执行，有些需要更长时间的 deliberation。语言模型已展现出类似的自适应"推理"能力，但将这种能力迁移到连续控制策略中一直具有挑战性，因为直接在语言空间中推理可能缺乏空间理解和精确运动所需的粒度。

Method: LMP将推理建模为带有自回归潜分布的变分推断，推导出潜空间强化学习技术来可微地优化其变分下界。具体包括：(1) LMP-π：学习一个自回归潜空间，在其中进行迭代、自适应的信息检索（类似记忆宫殿），从而产生控制策略；(2) LMP-tok：同一框架衍生的变长动作分词器。

Result: LMP-π在仿真和真实世界领域均取得强经验性能，展现出可解释的、自适应的测试时计算分配能力。LMP-tok显著提升了下游自回归策略的性能。

Conclusion: 这项工作通过变分推断的视角，为控制问题中的潜空间推理提供了新视角。结果表明，连续控制策略中的推理能力可以通过自回归潜空间中组织信息来涌现，无需依赖语言空间，而是类似于"记忆宫殿"的迭代检索机制。

Abstract: Human decision-making is highly flexible -- some actions are taken immediately; others require longer deliberation. Language models have exhibited a similar capacity for adaptive "reasoning." However, transferring this capability to continuous control policies has been challenging, as directly reasoning in language space may lack the granularity for spatial understanding and precise motions. In this work, we show that reasoning for control policies can emerge by organizing information in an autoregressive latent space reminiscent of a memory palace, where retrieval is iterative and adaptive. Our method, Latent Memory Palace (LMP), formulates reasoning as variational inference with an autoregressive latent distribution. We derive a latent-space reinforcement learning technique to tractably optimize its variational lower bound. The resulting policy, LMP-$π$, achieves strong empirical performance in simulation and real-world domains while exhibiting interpretable, adaptive allocation of test-time compute. We further show that the same framework yields a variable-length action tokenizer, LMP-$\texttt{tok}$, which significantly improves the performance of downstream autoregressive policies. Together, these results present a new perspective on latent reasoning for control through the lens of variational inference.

</details>


### [150] [Super Weights in LLMs and the Failure of Selective Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08733)
*Shreyas Subramanian,Adewale Akinfaderin,Akarsha Sehwag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现"超级权重"（Super Weights）虽然对模型性能至关重要，但单独训练这些参数会导致模型性能崩溃至随机水平，而LoRA等结构化低秩更新方法即使只更新极少参数也能成功，证明参数重要性不等同于参数可训练性，有效微调依赖于对整个层的结构化分解而非针对单个重要参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现存在"超级权重"——移除后会导致模型性能大幅下降的单个参数。本文旨在探究这些超级权重是否具有可训练性，即如果它们如此重要，单独训练它们是否有效。

Method: 在OLMo-1B和OLMo-7B模型上，分别对Super Weight坐标进行隔离训练（100到8,192个参数），扩展到局部邻域（最多36K参数），以及对比随机位置训练；同时使用vanilla LoRA（更新注意力权重矩阵中所有位置）和down_proj层上的LoRA，并进行了10次不同种子的消融实验，约束LoRA更新在Super Weight坐标对应位置。

Result: 隔离训练Super Weights导致准确率降至随机猜测水平；扩展到36K邻域参数无改善；随机位置训练反而优于基线；Vanilla LoRA仅用0.16%参数即成功；将LoRA更新约束在Super Weight坐标位置与无约束LoRA结果无统计显著差异。

Conclusion: 参数重要性不等同于参数可训练性，有效微调依赖于对整个层的结构化分解（如低秩结构），而非针对单个重要权重进行训练。

Abstract: Recent work identified Super Weights, individual parameters whose removal degrades model performance by orders of magnitude. We show that this degradation due to pruning Super Weights does not universally apply to all LLMs. Furthermore, if these parameters are so important, Super Weight-aware training should be effective. We show the opposite. Training Super Weights in isolation (100 to 8,192 parameters) drops accuracy to random-guessing levels on both OLMo-1B and OLMo-7B, and expanding to local neighborhoods of up to 36K parameters provides no improvement. The failure is specific to Super Weight coordinates: training an equal number of randomly chosen positions in the same down_proj layers instead improves over the baseline, so the collapse comes from targeting Super Weights, not from sparsity itself. Vanilla LoRA, updating every position in attention weight matrices through low-rank structure, succeeds with only 0.16% of parameters, and applying the same low-rank update to down_proj succeeds as well. A 10-seed ablation confirms that constraining LoRA updates at positions corresponding to Super Weight coordinates yields statistically indistinguishable results. These findings establish that parameter importance does not imply parameter trainability in isolation, and that effective fine-tuning relies on structured decompositions over entire layers rather than targeting individually important weights.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Tree-based solution representations for quadratic bilinear systems and their consequences in model order reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07841)
*Martin Redmann*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对二次双线性系统，提出了基于树结构的新解表示方法，将解分解为耦合双线性子系统的序列，并建立全局解存在的充分条件及输出界。在此基础上，定义了时间受限与无限时域的可达性/可观性格拉姆矩阵，通过非线性矩阵方程刻画其存在性条件，进而利用格拉姆矩阵核的去除实现精确降阶模型。最终提出平衡截断方法并推导误差界，统一了树表示、非线性格拉姆理论和平衡截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二次双线性系统的格拉姆矩阵基模型降阶理论存在若干理论空白，缺乏系统化的解表示框架与误差分析工具。

Method: 采用全二叉树索引的显式展开将解分解为耦合双线性子系统序列；定义时间受限/无限时域可达性和可观性格拉姆矩阵，通过非线性矩阵方程刻画存在性；利用去除格拉姆矩阵核实现精确降阶，在此基础上提出平衡截断方法。

Result: 建立了全局解存在的充分条件和新的输出界；给出了格拉姆矩阵存在的充分条件；提出了平衡截断降阶方法并证明了误差界。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了树结构解表示、非线性格拉姆矩阵理论和平衡截断，填补了二次双线性系统格拉姆矩阵基模型降阶的多项理论空白。

Abstract: We investigate quadratic bilinear systems by developing novel tree-based representations of their solutions. The proposed framework decomposes the solution into a sequence of coupled bilinear subsystems whose components admit explicit expansions indexed by full binary trees. These representations yield sufficient conditions for the existence of global solutions and lead to new output bounds in terms of reachability Gramians. Motivated by these estimates, we introduce time-limited and infinite-horizon reachability and observability Gramians, establish sufficient conditions for their existence, and characterize them through nonlinear matrix equations. The associated Gramians are employed to identify dominant state-spaces and to derive exact reduced-order models obtained by removing Gramian kernels. Building on these results, we develop a balanced truncation method for quadratic bilinear systems and prove an error bound for the reduced-order approximation. The proposed framework provides a unified connection between tree-based solution representations, nonlinear Gramian theory, and balanced truncation for quadratic bilinear systems, closing several theoretical gaps in the analysis of Gramian-based model reduction for this class of systems.

</details>


### [152] [On the Approximation of Optimal Control in Regime-Switching Diffusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07896)
*Somnath Pradhan,Dinesh Rathia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了受控体制切换扩散过程的最优控制策略的近似与结构简化，针对折扣、遍历、有限时域和退出时间等不同准则。通过建立代价泛函在马尔可夫策略类上的连续性，证明了存在有限动作、分段常数和Lipschitz连续的ε-最优策略。进一步构造了在分段常数控制下的Euler-Maruyama离散化方案，证明了强收敛性和有限时域值函数的收敛率O(h^{γ/2})，并基于状态空间量化发展了有限状态近似，证明了有限模型的值函数紧集上一致收敛以及最优策略的渐近最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受控体制切换扩散过程在金融、工程等领域有广泛应用，但其最优控制策略往往结构复杂、难以直接求解。为了数值实现和实际应用，需要研究策略的结构简化与近似方法，从而在保证性能的前提下降低计算复杂度。

Method: 利用椭圆和抛物正则性理论建立代价泛函在马尔可夫策略类上的连续性；利用有限动作、分段常数和Lipschitz连续策略的稠密性证明ε-最优策略的存在性；构造Euler-Maruyama离散化逼近受控体制切换扩散过程，并证明强收敛性和值函数收敛率；通过状态空间量化构建有限状态马尔可夫链近似，建立有限模型与原问题之间的收敛关系。

Result: 建立了代价泛函在马尔可夫和平稳马尔可夫策略类上的连续性；证明了在每个控制准则下均存在有限动作、分段常数和Lipschitz连续的ε-最优策略；分段常数控制下Euler-Maruyama离散化的强收敛性以及有限时域值函数的O(h^{γ/2})收敛率；有限状态近似模型的值函数在紧集上一致收敛于原问题的值函数，且近似模型的最优策略具有渐近最优性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个系统性的框架用于近似受控体制切换扩散控制问题，从理论上证明了结构化策略和有限状态模型的合理性，为数值实现奠定了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: We study approximation and structural simplification of optimal control policies for controlled regime-switching diffusion processes for discounted, ergodic, finite-horizon, and exit-time criteria. We first establish continuity of the cost functionals over classes of Markov and stationary Markov policies by exploiting elliptic and parabolic regularity of the corresponding Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman and Poisson equations. Using density results of policies with finite-action, piecewise-constant, and Lipschitz continuous, we show that each control problem admits $\varepsilon$-optimal policies within these structured subclasses. We then construct an Euler--Maruyama approximation of the controlled regime-switching diffusion under piecewise-constant controls. We prove strong convergence of the controlled state process and establish convergence of the associated finite-horizon value functions with rate $O(h^{γ/2})$. Building on this discretization, we develop a finite-state approximation of the induced discrete-time Markov chain via state-space quantization. We show that the value functions of the finite models converge uniformly on compact sets to the value function of the original problem, and that optimal policies of the approximating models are asymptotically optimal.
  These results provide a systematic framework for approximating regime-switching diffusion control problems and justify the use of structured policies and finite-state models for numerical implementation.

</details>


### [153] [Empirical Comparison of Unified Benders Cuts for Multi-Commodity Fixed-Charge Network Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07899)
*Eric Larsen,Jean-François Cordeau,Antonio Frangioni,Emma Frejinger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了50种Benders分解方法在多商品固定费用网络设计问题（MCFNDP）上的性能，重点比较了统一割（unified cuts）与不同割（distinct cuts）的生成策略，发现静态Brandenberg-Stursberg割和Hosseini-Turner l1-deepest割表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一割生成方案近年来在Benders分解加速技术中备受关注，但其在不同问题上的实际性能缺乏系统性的实验评估。本文旨在填补这一空白，重点评估统一割与不同割在多商品固定费用网络设计问题（MCFNDP）上的实验性能。

Method: 首先在统一的数学结构与符号下表达各类Benders割（统一割与不同割）的构造；然后将通用Benders割公式专门化以适应MCFNDP的规范；提出针对异构问题实例基准的求解方法性能比较方法；最后在标准化MCFNDP实例测试集上系统比较50种Benders方法，使用Gurobi和CPLEX作为SMS++计算库的支撑求解器。

Result: 在50种Benders方法中，识别出少数领先方法：静态Brandenberg-Stursberg割和Hosseini-Turner l1-deepest割表现最佳。同时报告了使用Gurobi和CPLEX作为SMS++计算库支撑求解器的实验结果。

Conclusion: 统一割生成方案中，静态Brandenberg-Stursberg割和Hosseini-Turner l1-deepest割在多商品固定费用网络设计问题上表现最优，为实际应用中的Benders分解策略选择提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Among the many types of acceleration techniques designed to improve the performance of Benders decomposition, unified cut generation schemes have recently attracted a keen interest. Unified cuts aim for a better balance between the generation of optimality and feasibility cuts, while also providing a way to compare the strengths of different feasibility cuts. Our goal is to assess the experimental performance of a broad selection of unified and distinct Benders cuts in the context of the multi-commodity fixed-charge network design problem (MCFNDP).
  We express under a common mathematical structure and notation the construction of each unified or distinct Benders cut considered. We also explain how the generic formulations of the Benders cuts can be specialized to conform to the specifications of the MCFNDP. In addition, we suggest bespoke methods for comparing the performance of several solution methods when the benchmark is made up of heterogeneous problem instances. We report the results of a systematic empirical analysis comparing the performances of 50 Benders methods involving unified or distinct cuts in applications to a common testing bench made up of standardized MCFNDP instances. The analysis identifies a small number of leading Benders methods, namely those featuring the static Brandenberg-Stursberg cuts and the Hosseini-Turner l1-deepest cuts. In addition, we also report results obtained by using both Gurobi and CPLEX as the supporting solver to the SMS++ computation library.

</details>


### [154] [Local Linear Convergence of the Primal-Dual Hybrid Gradient Method for Semidefinite Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08035)
*Xin Jiang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究原始-对偶混合梯度法（PDHG）在求解半定规划（SDP）时的局部收敛性，证明在严格互补性或原始-对偶非退化性条件下PDHG最终R-线性收敛，并给出一个两种条件均失效时只能次线性收敛的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然原始-对偶一阶方法广泛用于大规模SDP，但其计算高精度解的能力无法仅由全局收敛理论解释，需深入理解局部收敛行为。

Method: 将PDHG视为KKT包含的预条件邻近点方法，结合下降不等式与局部误差界分析。在严格互补性条件下利用正定锥的局部谱几何导出误差界；在原始-对偶非退化性条件下利用KKT映射的强正则性导出误差界。

Result: PDHG在满足严格互补性或原始-对偶非退化性的KKT点附近最终R-线性收敛；给出了两种条件均失效时仅能次线性收敛的SDP实例；与LP中PDHG即使在退化实例下也能线性收敛形成对比。

Conclusion: PDHG在SDP上的局部收敛性依赖于问题的正则性条件，数值实验证实了理论发现并识别了PDHG难以达到高精度的困难SDP实例。

Abstract: Primal-dual first-order methods are widely used for large-scale semidefinite programming (SDP), but their ability to compute highly accurate solutions is not well explained by global convergence theory alone. We study the local convergence of the primal-dual hybrid gradient (PDHG) method applied to a standard primal--dual SDP pair. We show that PDHG converges eventually (R-)linearly whenever the limiting KKT point satisfies either strict complementarity or primal--dual nondegeneracy. The proof views PDHG as a preconditioned proximal point method for the KKT inclusion and combines its descent inequality with a local error bound. Under strict complementarity, the error bound follows from the local spectral geometry of the positive semidefinite cone; under primal-dual nondegeneracy, it follows from strong regularity of the KKT mapping. We also give a simple SDP instance where both regularity conditions fail and PDHG can converge only sublinearly. This contrasts with linear programming, where PDHG admits a local linear convergence regime even for degenerate instances. Numerical experiments support the theory and identify difficult SDP instances where PDHG struggles to reach high accuracy.

</details>


### [155] [Sharp Spectral Bounds for Symmetric Positive Definite Tensors via Multiple Algebraic Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08113)
*Hemant Sharma,Snigdhashree Nayak,Ankit Singh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了对称正定张量H-特征值的迹-行列式框架，用精确优化解替代AM-GM松弛，引入高阶幂和作为谱不变量，并通过结构定理将问题降维为低维多项式系统，实现了对H-特征值的紧界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于AM-GM松弛的迹-行列式方法对H-特征值的上下界不够紧致，存在较大的估计间隙，需要更精确的界来改进谱分析及实际应用。

Method: 1) 用约束优化问题的精确解替代AM-GM松弛，得到可达的尖锐上下界；2) 引入高阶幂和作为额外谱不变量，证明在K-不变可行域上极值点至多只有K个不同谱值的结构定理；3) 将问题转化为有限个低维多项式系统，形成层次化紧界框架；4) 针对四不变量情形发展完整理论，包括解计数估计、多起始牛顿算法和尖锐性条件；5) 推导小维度闭式界、建立扰动估计、改进Lyapunov吸引域估计。

Result: 在维度高达d=100的数值实验中，尖锐的三不变量界将中位数相对过估计间隙从53%降低到6%，且计算成本低；方法在真实H-谱张量上得到验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于精确优化和谱不变量扩展的框架能显著改善H-特征值界的紧致性，兼具理论完备性和实际计算效率。

Abstract: We extend the trace--determinant framework of Nayak, Sharma, and Mishra~\cite{nayak2026} for bounding the H-eigenvalues of symmetric positive definite tensors. First, we replace the Arithmetic--Geometric Mean (AM--GM) relaxation underlying previous bounds by the exact solution of the associated constrained optimization problem, yielding sharp upper and lower bounds that are attained on the admissible spectral variety. Second, we incorporate higher-order power sums as additional spectral invariants and prove a structural theorem showing that any extremizer over a $K$-invariant feasibility region has at most $K$ distinct spectral values. This reduces the problem to a finite collection of low-dimensional polynomial systems and yields a hierarchy of increasingly tight bounds. For the four-invariant case $(T,S,p_3,D)$, we develop a complete theory including solution-count estimates, a multistart Newton algorithm, and sharpness conditions. We also derive closed-form bounds in small dimensions, establish perturbation estimates, and obtain refined Lyapunov region-of-attraction bounds. Numerical experiments for dimensions up to $d=100$ show that the sharp three-invariant bound reduces the median relative overestimation gap from $53\%$ to $6\%$ while maintaining low computational cost. The framework is validated on tensors with real H-spectrum.

</details>


### [156] [Accelerated Golden Ratio Primal--Dual Algorithm for Structured Convex Optimisation without Linesearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08174)
*Santanu Soe,V. Vetrivel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Soe等人(2026)提出的自适应扩展黄金比例原始-对偶算法(aEGRPDA)，证明了其原始步长上界是冗余的，从而得到无需步长上限、线搜索或全局Lipschitz常数的自适应黄金比例原始-对偶方法，并建立了线性收敛性以及两种加速变体的O(1/N²)收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应扩展黄金比例原始-对偶算法(aEGRPDA)对原始步长施加了人为上界，该超参数的存在增加了调参负担；本文旨在证明该上界是冗余的，从而简化算法并提升实用性。

Method: 本文通过理论分析证明aEGRPDA中原始步长的人为上界是冗余的（自适应规则自身保持步长有界），进而提出无需步长上限、线搜索或全局Lipschitz常数的自适应黄金比例原始-对偶方法；在此基础上，针对局部光滑假设下的两种加速场景（非光滑原始分量强凸和可微项全局强凸）设计了加速变体。

Result: 证明了原始步长上界的冗余性，给出目标残差和可行性的遍历O(1/N)估计（独立于该超参数）；当原始和对偶函数均强凸时建立线性收敛性；加速变体达到遍历O(1/N²)收敛率；在泊松成像问题上的数值实验验证了效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应黄金比例原始-对偶方法消除了对步长上限、线搜索及全局Lipschitz常数的依赖，保留了收敛保证，并能在特定强凸条件下实现加速收敛，为结构凸优化问题提供了更简洁高效的求解方案。

Abstract: This paper revisits the adaptive extended golden-ratio primal--dual algorithm (aEGRPDA) proposed by Soe et al. (2026) for structured convex optimisation problems involving a differentiable term that is only locally smooth. We prove that the artificial upper bound imposed on the primal step-size in aEGRPDA is redundant, since the adaptive rule itself keeps the step-sizes bounded above. As a consequence, the ergodic $\mathcal O(1/N)$ estimates for the objective residual and feasibility violation, where $N\ge1$ denotes the number of iterations, are independent of this hyperparameter. Consequently, the resulting adaptive golden-ratio primal--dual method, therefore, requires neither a step-size cap, nor a linesearch procedure, nor a known global Lipschitz constant. We establish linear convergence of the algorithm when both the primal and dual functions are strongly convex. Furthermore, we develop two accelerated variants, in addition to the local smoothness assumption: one for the case where the nonsmooth primal component is strongly convex, and another for the case where the differentiable term is globally strongly convex. For these accelerated methods, we prove an ergodic $\mathcal O(1/N^2)$ convergence rate. Preliminary numerical experiments on a Poisson imaging problem illustrate the efficiency and robustness of the proposed approaches.

</details>


### [157] [On the stability of proximal operators in Wasserstein spaces under different notions of convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08209)
*Simone Di Marino,Sara Farinelli,Emanuele Naldi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究Wasserstein空间中不同凸性概念下近端算子的非扩张性（1-Lipschitz连续性）等稳定性性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希尔伯特空间中，真下半连续凸泛函的近端算子具有非扩张性，但Wasserstein空间中该算子的收缩性质尚未完全理解，已有研究（Carlen和Craig、Adve和Mészáros等）仍需进一步澄清。

Method: 从Wasserstein空间中可考虑的各种凸性概念出发，系统研究近端映射的稳定性性质，重点关注非扩张性条件。

Result: 揭示了在不同凸性定义下近端算子的收缩性质，特别是非扩张性成立的充分条件或反例。

Conclusion: 对Wasserstein空间中近端算子的Lipschitz连续性给出了更完整的刻画，推进了该领域理论理解。

Abstract: The proximal operator is a fundamental tool in variational analysis and optimization. In the setting of a Hilbert space, given a proper, lower semicontinuous convex functional, its proximal operator is non-expansive, that is, 1-Lipschitz continuous. In the Wasserstein setting, the contraction properties of this operator have been investigated from different perspectives by Carlen and Craig and Adve and Mészáros, among others, and are not completely understood. In this paper, we study the stability properties of proximal maps, with a particular focus on non-expansivity, under various notions of convexity of the functional that can be considered in the Wasserstein space.

</details>


### [158] [Sub-Infinite Horizon Stochastic Linear-Quadratic Optimal Control Problems and Delayed Backward Riccati Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08225)
*Yutao Chen,Hongwei Lou,Hanxiao Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一类次无限时域随机线性二次最优控制问题，发现标准方法得到的最优控制是时间不一致的，因此提出了一种新的带延迟项的Riccati方程来求解时间一致、局部最优且时不变的均衡策略，并通过先验估计和Leray-Schauder不动点定理解决了该方程的适定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在次无限时域随机线性二次最优控制问题中，标准方法得到的最优控制通常是时间不一致的，无法在实际应用中持续采用。因此需要寻找一种时间一致、局部最优且时不变的均衡策略。

Method: 引入了一类新型Riccati方程，其生成元包含未知量的延迟项，本质上是一个带延迟的向后常微分方程（等价于带超前项的向前ODE），进而转化为Fredholm积分方程。通过建立尖锐的先验估计并应用Leray-Schauder不动点定理，同时建立两个矩阵值非线性代数方程之间的比较定理，证明了方程的可解性。

Result: 成功得到了时间一致、局部最优且时不变的均衡策略；证明了带延迟项Riccati方程的解的存在性；给出了当T→∞时Riccati方程解的收敛行为。

Conclusion: 本文为次无限时域随机线性二次最优控制问题提供了一种求解时间一致均衡策略的有效方法，所提出的带延迟Riccati方程及其求解方法具有理论意义和应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a class of so-called sub-infinite horizon stochastic linear-quadratic optimal control problems, in which the initial time $t$ is arbitrarily taken from $[0,\infty)$ and the running cost is defined over $[t,t+T]$ for a given $T>0$. The optimal control of this type of problem can be obtained by standard methods; however, it is shown that the resulting optimal control is generally time-inconsistent. Thus, instead of seeking an optimal control, which is time-inconsistent, we aim to find a time-consistent, locally optimal, and time-invariant equilibrium strategy, by introducing a new and very interesting type of Riccati equation. Its main feature is that the generator depends on a delay term of the unknown. In other words, this Riccati equation is a backward ordinary differential equation (ODE) with delay, which is equivalent to a forward ODE with advanced terms. Such an equation is essentially a Fredholm integral equation, whose solvability is challenging. We overcome the difficulty by deriving a sharp a priori estimate and applying the Leray--Schauder fixed point theorem. To this end, we establish a comparison theorem between two matrix-valued nonlinear algebraic equations. The convergence behavior of the solution to the Riccati equation as $T\to\infty$ is also provided.

</details>


### [159] [Projected incrementally scattering passive systems on closed convex sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08301)
*Shantanu Singh,Sébastien Fueyo,George Weiss*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了将增量散射无源系统的状态限制到状态空间的闭凸子集K上得到的投影动力系统，仍然是增量散射无源系统。首先证明了最大耗散算子在K的切锥上的投影仍然是最大耗散的，因此它决定了压缩半群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究投影动力系统在状态空间闭凸子集上的无源性保持性质，为约束条件下的无源系统分析提供理论基础。

Method: 通过分析最大耗散算子在闭凸子集K的切锥上的投影性质，证明其最大耗散性保持不变，进而推导压缩半群的存在性和系统的增量散射无源性。

Result: 投影后的动力系统仍然是增量散射无源系统；投影算子保持最大耗散性，并决定一个压缩半群。

Conclusion: 状态约束下的投影操作不会破坏增量散射无源系统的核心结构性质，为约束无源系统的分析与综合提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: In this article we show that the projected dynamical system obtained by restricting the state of an incrementally scattering passive system to a closed and convex subset K of the state space (a real Hilbert space), is also an incrementally scattering passive system. First we show that the projection of a maximal dissipative operator to the tangent cones of K is again maximal dissipative, hence, it determines a contraction semigroup.

</details>


### [160] [Controllability and Exponential Mixing in Singular Interacting Particle Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08435)
*Laurent Mertz,Vahagn Nersesyan,Manuel Rissel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有奇异核的相互作用粒子系统，在确定性退化解耦控制下建立了全局精确可控性和一种称为"固体可控性"的拓扑鲁棒性质，同时证明了具有预定轨迹的全局近似可控性；在随机动力学中，基于可控性通过耦合与递归机制获得了遍历性和指数混合性。该方法利用奇异性，适用于Biot-Savart、Coulomb、Riesz、Yukawa相互作用及异质多物种系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论对具有奇异核的相互作用粒子系统的可控性和遍历性研究不足，特别是同时处理确定性退化控制和随机退化噪声的情形，亟需建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 利用核的奇异性设计控制策略（确定性情形），以及基于可控性构造耦合与递归机制（随机情形）来证明遍历性和指数混合性。

Result: 确定性：全局精确可控性+固体可控性+预定轨迹的全局近似可控性。随机：遍历性+指数混合性。

Conclusion: 该方法利用内在奇异性，为Biot-Savart、Coulomb、Riesz、Yukawa等多种奇异核相互作用系统建立了统一的可控性与遍历性理论。

Abstract: This article concerns interacting particle systems with singular kernels, driven either by degenerate deterministic controls or by degenerate decomposable noise. In the deterministic setting, we establish global exact controllability and a topologically robust property called solid controllability. Moreover, we prove a result that guarantees global approximate controllability with prescribed trajectories. For stochastic dynamics, we obtain ergodicity and exponential mixing by utilizing coupling and recurrence mechanisms based on controllability. Our approach exploits the singularity and applies to a broad class of models, including Biot-Savart, Coulomb, Riesz, and Yukawa interactions, as well as heterogeneous multi-species systems.

</details>


### [161] [Second order systems on Hilbert spaces with nonlinear damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08506)
*Shantanu Singh,George Weiss*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类通过修改M. Tucsnak和G. Weiss在2003年提出的保守线性系统二阶微分方程而得到的非线性无穷维系统，其中引入了一个极大单调且可能集值的新非线性阻尼项。利用极大单调算子理论和Crandall-Pazy定理关于非线性压缩半群的结果，证明了该类系统是增量散射无源的（因此是适定的），并以n维波动方程为例进行了说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将线性保守系统扩展至非线性领域，研究含非线性阻尼项的无穷维系统的适定性和无源性。

Method: 采用极大单调算子理论和Crandall-Pazy定理，将Lax-Phillips型非线性半群应用于整个系统。

Result: 证明了该类非线性无穷维系统是增量散射无源的，从而保证了系统的适定性。

Conclusion: 成功构建了一类带极大单调非线性阻尼的无穷维系统，并验证了其无源性和适定性，n维波动方程实例支持了理论结果。

Abstract: We investigate a special class of nonlinear infinite dimensional systems. These systems are obtained by modifying the second order differential equation that is part of the description of conservative linear systems out of thin air introduced by M. Tucsnak and G. Weiss in 2003. The modified differential equation contains a new nonlinear damping term, that is maximal monotone and possibly set-valued. We show that this new class of nonlinear infinite dimensional systems is incrementally scattering passive (hence well-posed). Our approach uses the theory of maximal monotone operators and the Crandall-Pazy theorem about nonlinear contraction semigroups, which we apply to a Lax-Phillips type nonlinear semigroup that represents the whole system. We illustrate our result on the n-dimensional wave equation.

</details>


### [162] [Preconditioned primal-dual algorithms for saddle point problems: non-ergodic convergence rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08633)
*Huiyuan Guo,Juan José Maulén,Juan Peypouquet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类面向凸-凹鞍点问题的预条件原始-对偶算法，利用光滑+非光滑结构，提出反对称预条件子以实现非遍历收敛速率，并通过数值实验验证算法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展预条件原始-对偶动力学框架，使其适用于更广泛的凸-凹鞍点问题（包括线性约束凸优化），并考虑计算误差的影响。

Method: 基于Apidopoulos等人提出的动力学框架，引入反对称预条件子，利用光滑+非光滑结构，在存在计算误差的情况下建立非遍历收敛速率。

Result: 提出的预条件原始-对偶算法在非遍历意义下具有收敛保证，且数值实验表明算法性能良好。

Conclusion: 反对称预条件子能有效提升凸-凹鞍点问题的求解效率，且算法对计算误差具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We study a family of preconditioned primal dual algorithms for convex-concave saddle point problems by the dynamics introduced in \cite{apidopoulos2026preconditioned}. The proposed framework exploits the possible smooth + nonsmooth structure of the saddle point formulation. It includes, but is not limited to, linearly constrained convex optimization problems. The proposed antisymmetric preconditioners allow us to establish non ergodic convergence rates, accounting for possible computational errors in the implementation of the method. Finally, we present numerical experiments to indicate our well performed preconditioned primal dual algorithms.

</details>


### [163] [Mathematical methods of reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06935)
*Denis Belomestny,Alexander Gasnikov,Egor Gladin,Alexey Naumov,Artemy Rubtsov,Yuri Sapronov,Daniil Tiapkin,Nikita Yudin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了强化学习中的数学基础结构，包括马尔可夫决策过程、贝尔曼算子、随机逼近、凸对偶、函数逼近和约束RL等，为来自概率、优化和统计学领域的研究者提供了统一的数学入门点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习日益依赖于概率、优化和算子理论等数学工具，但缺乏一个统一的数学视角来组织这些基础结构，使得来自相关领域的研究者难以系统性地进入RL领域。

Method: 通过综述形式，从马尔可夫决策过程和贝尔曼算子出发，依次涵盖压缩映射、不动点理论、随机逼近与鞅方法、凸对偶与正则化、线性/非线性函数逼近、离策略评估与学习、约束马尔可夫决策过程等主题，并以算子视角和变分视角统一各算法模板。

Result: 建立了强化学习算法设计与分析的统一数学框架，涵盖了收敛性保证、收敛速率、有限样本界和渐近结果等理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文为概率、优化和统计学领域的研究者提供了一个统一的强化学习数学入门指南，有助于跨学科研究者理解和推进RL的理论基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly grounded in tools from probability, optimization, and operator theory. This survey organizes the mathematical structures that underpin the design and analysis of modern algorithms in RL. We begin from Markov decision processes (MDPs) and the Bellman operators, emphasizing contraction mappings, monotonicity, and fixed-point theory that yield convergence guarantees and rates for value and policy iteration, and temporal-difference schemes. We then develop the optimization perspective: stochastic approximation and martingale methods, convex duality and the role of regularization linking mirror/proximal methods. Function approximation is treated through linear and non-linear settings, covering stabilization, error decomposition, and sample-complexity via concentration inequalities for dependent data and mixing processes. We further cover off-policy evaluation/learning, constrained RL and constrained MDPs (CMDPs). Throughout we unify algorithmic templates under common operator and variational lenses, highlighting both finite-sample bounds and asymptotic results. Our presentation is intended to provide a unified mathematical entry point for researchers in probability, optimization, and statistics interested in reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [164] [Aclass of incrementally scattering-passive nonlinear systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08637)
*Shantanu Singh,George Weiss,Marius Tucsnak*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类非线性无穷维系统，通过从散射被动线性系统的半群生成子中减去一个非线性极大单调（可能是多值）算子M得到。证明了该类系统是适定的且增量散射被动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将线性散射被动系统扩展到非线性情形，处理可能具有无界线性阻尼（如边界阻尼）的系统，其中非线性阻尼算子M定义在整个状态空间上。

Method: 利用极大单调算子理论和Crandall-Pazy定理关于非线性压缩半群的结果，将其应用于代表整个系统的Lax-Phillips型非线性半群。

Result: 证明了该新类非线性无穷维系统是适定的（well-posed）且具有增量散射被动性（incrementally scattering passive）。

Conclusion: 成功构建了一类特殊的非线性无穷维系统，并建立了其适定性和增量散射被动性，为非线性散射系统理论提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: We investigate a special class of nonlinear infinite dimensional systems. These are obtained by subtracting a nonlinear maximal monotone (possibly multi-valued) operator M from the semigroup generator of a scattering passive linear system. While the linear system may have unbounded linear damping (for instance, boundary damping) which is only densely defined, the nonlinear damping operator M is assumed to be defined on the whole state space. We show that this new class of nonlinear infinite dimensional systems is well-posed and incrementally scattering passive. Our approach uses the theory of maximal monotone operators and the Crandall-Pazy theorem about nonlinear contraction semigroups, which we apply to a Lax-Phillips type nonlinear semigroup that represents the whole system.

</details>


### [165] [Sharp bounds for stochastic proximal and projection estimators via radial dominance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08670)
*Gonzalo Contador,Pedro Pérez-Aros,Emilio Vilches*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究随机重心估计量用于近端点和度量投影，通过指数重新加权高斯扰动得到。提出了径向占优条件下概率测度的抽象比较定理，给出了重心范数的显式上界，并获得了弱凸函数随机近端估计量的精细收敛速率及常数的渐近sharpness，以及闭凸集随机投影估计量的相应速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立随机重心估计量（针对近端点和度量投影）的理论分析框架，获得收敛速率和常数渐近sharpness的理论保证。

Method: 通过指数重新加权高斯扰动构造随机重心估计量，提出径向占优条件下的抽象比较定理，利用一维比较测度给出重心范数的上界，并提供强凸性、非负凸项加法和星形约束等可处理的充分条件。

Result: 获得了弱凸函数随机近端估计量的精细收敛速率及常数的渐近sharpness；得到了闭凸集随机投影估计量的相应速率；建立了重心逼近算子的光滑性和co-coercivity等结构性质。

Conclusion: 随机重心估计量在近端点和投影问题上具有理论保障的良好收敛性质，数值实验验证了预测速率、维度缩放常数及其渐近sharpness。

Abstract: We study stochastic barycentric estimators for proximal points and metric projections obtained by exponentially reweighting Gaussian perturbations. Our main result is an abstract comparison theorem for probability measures with densities proportional to an exponential weight, under a radial dominance condition relative to a prescribed profile. This yields an explicit upper bound for the norm of the associated barycenter in terms of a one-dimensional comparison measure. We also provide tractable sufficient conditions for radial dominance, including strong convexity, addition of nonnegative convex terms, and star-shaped constraints.
  As a consequence, we obtain a refined convergence rate for stochastic proximal estimators of weakly convex functions, together with asymptotic sharpness of the constant. The same framework yields a corresponding rate for stochastic projection estimators onto closed convex sets. We further establish basic structural properties of the barycentric approximation operator, such as smoothness and cocoercivity. Numerical experiments illustrate the predicted rate, the dimensional scaling of the constant, and its asymptotic sharpness.

</details>


### [166] [Finding Simple Proofs for First-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08753)
*Daniel Berg Thomsen,Manu Upadhyaya,Baptiste Goujaud,Aymeric Dieuleveut,Adrien Taylor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从密集的机器生成证明中提取简洁、可解释证明结构的后处理方法。通过将问题建模为二阶优化问题，利用稀疏优化和统计学习工具对拉格朗日对偶证书进行修剪，从而发现简单证明模式与中间引理（如Lyapunov函数），并在梯度下降、近端方法和快速梯度方法上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学进步不仅需要真理性证明，还需要透明、可检验、可复用的证明结构。现有的自动证明系统虽然能验证结果的正确性，但输出的通常是密集的证书而非可解释的证明结构，这阻碍了人工理解、复用和形式化。

Method: 将寻找更简单证明结构视为二阶优化问题，从对偶证书出发，采用稀疏优化和统计学习工具进行后处理。具体方法包括：穷举稀疏化、加权ℓ1类型启发式方法以及基于半定规划（SDP）的公式化方法，通过衡量假设活跃度和残差结构等特征来发现简单证明和中间引理。

Result: 在梯度下降、近端方法和快速梯度方法上的实验表明，该方法能够自动修剪冗余不等式、揭示结构化证明模式，并在近端设置中恢复Lyapunov函数作为中间引理，从而得到简洁、流畅的证明。

Conclusion: 该工作通过将密集的机器生成证书提炼为紧凑的证明结构，可作为最终证明的预处理步骤，降低人类在解释、复用和形式化过程中需要处理的复杂性。

Abstract: Progress in mathematics often requires more than a certificate of truth: it requires proof structures that are transparent, checkable, and reusable. Automated systems can increasingly certify that a result is true; what they typically return, however, is a dense certificate rather than an interpretable, reusable proof structure.
  Recent work on performance estimation problems has shown that performance bounds and complexity analyses of first-order optimization methods can be discovered by searching over a structured space of Lagrangian dual certificates. We cast the search for simpler proof structures as a second-stage optimization problem over these certificates. Starting from dual certificates, we develop post-processing procedures using tools from sparse optimization and statistical learning. We measure complexity through features such as active hypotheses and residual structure, and introduce methods based on exhaustive sparsification, weighted $\ell_1$-type heuristics, and semidefinite programming (SDP) formulations for discovering simple proofs and intermediate lemmas.
  Examples on gradient descent, proximal methods, and fast-gradient methods show that these procedures can autonomously prune redundant inequalities, reveal structured proof patterns, and, in the proximal setting, recover Lyapunov functions as intermediate lemmas that lead to simple, streamlined proofs. By distilling dense machine-generated certificates into compact proof structures, this workflow acts as a pre-processing step for the final proof, reducing the complexity that must be managed during human interpretation, reuse, and formalization.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [The Regularization Parameter: Sparse Precision Matrix Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07735)
*Aryan Eftekhari,Daniel Sergio Vega,Ernst-Jan Camiel Wit,Olaf Schenk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种闭式矩阵值正则化参数，基于ℓ1正则化高斯极大似然估计的一阶最优性条件的采样分布，无需交叉验证即可实现精度矩阵估计，在合成数据和真实应用中达到与交叉验证相当的估计精度、更优的支持恢复，并大幅降低计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维低样本量数据中，稀疏精度矩阵估计的关键挑战在于如何恰当选择控制稀疏度的正则化参数，以平衡欠拟合与过拟合，而传统交叉验证方法计算成本高。

Method: 从ℓ1正则化高斯极大似然估计的一阶最优性条件的采样分布出发，推导出闭式矩阵值正则化参数。通过规定在重采样下每个非零估计量条目满足其最优性条件的概率，取消了交叉验证需求。

Result: 在标准条件下，所提正则化参数具有渐近尺度性质，保证了估计量的一致性和稀疏一致性。在合成高斯/非高斯数据、基因微阵列和神经影像应用中，估计精度与交叉验证相当，支持恢复更优，运行时间降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种无需交叉验证、计算高效的稀疏精度矩阵估计方案，在理论保证和实证表现上均有竞争力。

Abstract: Sparse precision matrix estimation provides an interpretable and computationally efficient framework for modeling conditional dependencies in high-dimensional, low-sample-size data. A recurring challenge is appropriately selecting the regularization parameter that controls estimator sparsity and strikes a balance between underfitting and overfitting. We propose a closed-form, matrix-valued regularization parameter derived from the sampling distribution of the first-order optimality conditions of the $\ell_1$-regularized Gaussian maximum-likelihood estimator. By prescribing the probability that each nonzero entry of the estimator satisfies its optimality condition under resampling, we eliminate the need for cross-validation. The resulting regularization parameter is shown to attain asymptotic scaling properties that, under standard conditions, provide consistency and sparsistency of the estimator. On synthetic Gaussian and non-Gaussian datasets, as well as real-world gene microarray and neuroimaging applications, the proposed approach achieves estimation accuracy comparable to cross-validation, delivers superior support recovery, and reduces runtime by several orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [168] [Distributionally Faithful Imputation via Positive Semi-Definite Kernel Density Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07767)
*Andrea Basteri,Carlo Ciliberto,Alessandro Rudi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 将缺失值插补问题重新定义为基于掩码观测的密度估计，利用PSD核密度提出凸经验风险问题，通过牛顿内点法求解，实现单重和多重插补，并具有统计一致性和快速自适应风险收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有插补方法依赖启发式或参数假设，忽略联合数据分布，缺乏原则性的统计框架。

Method: 将MCAR下的插补视为从掩码观测中估计密度；利用PSD核密度构造凸经验风险问题，其边际分布有闭式解；通过牛顿内点法求解。

Result: 在1个合成和11个真实数据集上，分布准确性与流行插补基线方法相当。

Conclusion: PSD Impute模型实现了单重与多重插补的统一框架，具有统计一致性和超越维数诅咒的收敛速度，展现出强实践潜力。

Abstract: Missing values undermine statistical inference and machine learning pipelines, yet most imputation methods rely on heuristics or restrictive parametric assumptions that ignore the joint data distribution. We recast imputation under missing completely at random (MCAR) as density estimation from masked observations: estimate a distribution whose observed marginals exactly match those in the data. Leveraging positive semi definite (PSD) kernel densities we obtain a convex empirical risk problem with closed form marginals, solvable by a Newton interior point method. The resulting PSD Impute model yields both single and multiple imputations from the same fitted density, enjoys statistical consistency with fast adaptive excess risk beating the curse of dimensionality for very regular probabilities. Preliminary experiments on one synthetic and eleven real world datasets already indicate competitive distributional accuracy compared with popular imputation baselines, suggesting strong practical promise.

</details>


### [169] [Expressivity and Statistical Trade-offs in Diffusion Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07967)
*Viet Vu,Renyuan Xu,Jiacheng Zhang,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散策略的Lipschitz预算K如何控制其表达能力和统计行为，证明了K越大表达能力越强（近似误差1/K），但统计复杂度也越高，最终得到有限样本性能差距为Õ(n^{-2/(m+6)})或Õ(n^{-2/(m+4)})，建议根据样本量选择K并固定神经网络Lipschitz系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在实践中表现出强大的表达灵活性，能建模复杂多模态非高斯动作分布，但数学上驱动这种表达性的因素尚不明确，且如何在有限数据下充分利用这种表达性也是一个未解决的问题。

Method: 辨识漂移Lipschitz预算K为核心量，从近似能力（上界1/K和下界匹配）和统计复杂度（神经网络参数化下的泛化误差）两方面刻画扩散策略，并推导有限样本性能界。

Result: 近似误差上界为1/K并匹配下界；神经网络漂移下有限样本性能界为Õ(n^{-2/(m+6)})，单边耗散类可改进至Õ(n^{-2/(m+4)})。数值实验验证了K的样本依赖权衡。

Conclusion: 提出实用原则：根据样本量选择扩散预算K，再选取对应固定Lipschitz系数的神经网络架构。

Abstract: Diffusion-based policies have recently emerged as powerful policy parameterizations for reinforcement learning, representing state-conditioned action distributions as terminal laws of diffusion processes with parameterized drifts. This terminal-law representation has shown substantial expressive flexibility in practice, enabling diffusion policies to model complex, multimodal, and highly non-Gaussian action distributions; however, it remains unclear what mathematically drives this expressivity and how to fully exploit it when the policy is learned from finite data. In this paper, we identify the drift Lipschitz budget $K$ as a central quantity governing the expressivity and statistical behavior of diffusion policies. We quantify expressivity through approximation: diffusion policies with $K$-Lipschitz drifts can concentrate near optimal deterministic policies and achieve value approximation error of order $1/K$; moreover, we prove a matching lower bound under nondegenerate diffusion noise. This increased expressivity comes with a statistical cost. When the drift is parameterized by neural networks, increasing $K$ improves approximation but increases statistical complexity. Balancing these two terms yields a finite-sample performance gap of order $\tilde{O}(n^{-2/(m+6)})$ for generic neural-network drifts, and a sharper rate $\tilde{O}(n^{-2/(m+4)})$ for one-sided dissipative drift classes, where $n$ is the sample size and $m$ is the dimension of the state space. Numerical experiments provide empirical evidence for the sample-dependent trade-off in $K$, supporting both theoretical regimes. Our framework also suggests a practical implementation principle: choose the diffusion budget $K$ according to the available sample size, and then select a neural-network architecture with the corresponding fixed Lipschitz coefficient.

</details>


### [170] [Bayesian Experimental Design via Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08335)
*Angus Phillips,Gavin Kerrigan,Tom Rainforth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将期望信息增益（EIG）的双重难解性与策略学习分离，通过先求解与策略无关的分数匹配问题，再使用学习到的分数近似以单重难解方式训练策略，将关键乘法成本转化为加法成本，显著降低贝叶斯实验设计中策略训练的计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于策略的贝叶斯实验设计方法中，策略网络训练面临EIG的双重难解性这一根本挑战，导致需要昂贵或复杂的近似，限制了策略优化的投入。

Method: 将EIG的双重难解性与策略学习解耦：首先求解一个与策略无关的分数匹配问题，然后利用学到的分数近似以单重难解的方式训练策略，从而将乘法成本转化为加法成本。

Result: 在实验中，无需引入似然评估的乘法成本即可训练多个竞争性策略，即使不进行超参数或架构搜索，也能通过选择最佳策略提升性能。

Conclusion: 该方法有效降低了策略训练的计算负担，使得在需要进行架构搜索、超参数调优或避免局部最优时，可以更廉价地多次训练策略。

Abstract: Policy-based approaches to Bayesian experimental design (BED) allow the learning of deep policy networks that adaptively make intelligent design decisions based on previously collected data. However, the training of such policies is often held back by a fundamental challenge: the double intractability of the expected information gain (EIG). This necessitates expensive or complex approximations that restrict the effort one can invest in optimising the policy itself. To address this, we show that the double intractability of the EIG can be isolated from the policy learning by first solving a score matching problem that is independent of the policy used, then using the learned score approximation to train the policy in a singly intractable manner. This turns the key multiplicative cost into an additive one and reduces the computational burden on the policy training itself, making it far cheaper to train the policy multiple times when needed, e.g. for architecture search, hyperparameter tuning, or avoiding local optima. In our experiments we train multiple competitive policies without inducing a multiplicative cost in likelihood evaluations, which can increase performance by allowing us to select the best policy even without performing hyperparameter or architecture searches.

</details>


### [171] [Prediction-Powered Active Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08347)
*Kianoosh Ashouritaklimi,Valentin Kilian,Daolang Huang,Tom Rainforth,François Caron*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出PPAT框架，利用预测驱动控制变量法实现标签高效的风险估计，在保持无偏性的同时降低方差，并推导了最优采集规则和渐近正态性，在表格回归和图像分类任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动测试方法未能有效利用强大黑盒模型的预测信息来提升标签效率，即使这些预测在标签昂贵的场景中已广泛可用。

Method: 将无偏LURE估计器与预测驱动控制变量结合，用预测残差化损失函数；推导了面向方差缩减的oracle和基于替代的采集规则；建立了渐近正态性理论保证。

Result: 在表格回归和图像分类任务中，PPAT在风险估计上优于现有方法，其置信区间能以更少的标签和更小的宽度达到目标覆盖率。

Conclusion: PPAT通过预测驱动控制变量有效利用了黑盒模型信息，在保持无偏性的同时显著提升了标签效率和估计精度，为标签稀缺场景下的风险估计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Active testing provides a label--efficient approach to risk estimation by adaptively selecting which test points should be labelled. However, existing estimators fail to exploit the informative predictions of powerful black--box models, even though such predictions are increasingly available in settings where labels remain expensive. To address this, we propose \textbf{Prediction--Powered Active Testing (PPAT)}, a novel label--efficient risk estimation framework that combines the unbiased LURE estimator \citep{farquhar2021statistical} with a prediction--powered control variate. Rather than using proxy predictions as biased pseudo--labels, PPAT uses them to residualise the loss, preserving unbiasedness while reducing variance. Beyond the estimator itself, PPAT also changes which points should be acquired: we derive oracle and practical surrogate--based acquisition rules tailored to reducing the variance of our estimator. Moreover, we establish asymptotic normality for PPAT, yielding asymptotically valid confidence intervals and thus a principled estimate of the uncertainty around our estimates. Across tabular regression and image--classification tasks, PPAT outperforms existing methods in risk estimation, while its confidence intervals attain the target coverage with substantially fewer labels and smaller widths.

</details>


### [172] [Statistical Efficiency and Inference of Quantile Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08444)
*Zijie Cheng,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从统计效率角度研究了基于分位数的分布式强化学习，重点分析了分布策略评估问题。在生成模型假设下，建立了分位数固定点估计的非渐近误差界（̃O(√(m/n))），证明了估计量的渐近正态性和半参数效率界，并在分位数数量趋于无穷时仍保持渐近效率，最后建立了平滑泛函的Berry-Esseen定理为统计推断提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从统计效率的角度严格分析基于分位数的分布式强化学习方法，特别是分布策略评估问题中的参数估计精度和渐近最优性。

Method: 利用分位数投影的分布式贝尔曼方程定义分位数不动点η_m；基于生成模型下的经验MDP构建估计量η_m^{(n)}；采用supremum W_∞度量分析误差；推导渐近分布与半参数效率界；研究分位数数量发散时的极限协方差结构；建立Berry-Esseen定理。

Result: 估计误差为̃O(√(m/n))，达到最优参数√n收敛速率；估计量达到半参数效率界；在分位数数量趋于无穷时仍保持渐近效率；建立了平滑泛函的Berry-Esseen定理。

Conclusion: 基于分位数的分布式策略评估可以在固定维度和无限维度下均实现统计最优，为分布强化学习提供了坚实的统计理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study quantile-based distributional reinforcement learning from the perspective of statistical efficiency. We focus on distributional policy evaluation, whose goal is to characterize the return distribution, namely the distribution of discounted cumulative rewards under a given policy. To obtain a finite-dimensional representation of the return distribution, we consider the quantile fixed point $η_m$ induced by the quantile-projected distributional Bellman equation. Assuming access to a generative model, we construct an estimator $η_m^{(n)}$ based on an empirical Markov decision process. For a fixed number of quantiles $m$, we establish a non-asymptotic error bound for $η_m^{(n)}$ and $η_m$ under the supremum $W_\infty$ metric, showing that the estimation error scales as $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{m/n})$ with respect to $m$ and $n$. This implies that the quantile-based distributional policy evaluation problem can be solved with sample efficiency, achieving the optimal parametric $\sqrt{n}$ convergence rate. We derive the asymptotic distribution of the quantile parameters $\sqrt{n}(θ_m^{(n)}-θ_m)$ and characterize the semiparametric efficiency bound, which is attained by our estimator. Beyond the fixed-dimensional setting, we investigate the asymptotic regime in which the number of quantiles diverges. We characterize the limit covariance structure and show that it matches the semiparametric efficiency bound of the nonparametric model for distributional policy evaluation, showing that quantile-based estimators remain asymptotically efficient in the infinite-dimensional limit. Finally, we establish a Berry--Esseen theorem for smooth functionals $\sqrt{n}(η_m^{(n)}(s)-η_m(s))f$, thereby providing a foundation for statistically valid inference on functionals of the quantile-projected return distribution.

</details>


### [173] [High-Dimensional Procrustes Matching via Tree Counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08538)
*Xiaochun Niu,Tselil Schramm,Jiaming Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维Procrustes匹配问题，提出了一种基于计数特定"宽"树的多项式时间算法，能在常数相关性下实现精确恢复，并给出了信息论下界和低度优势分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维场景（d ≫ log n）下的Procrustes匹配问题此前缺乏有效算法，现有方法要求近乎完美的相关性（ρ=1-o(1)）才能保证恢复，本文旨在突破这一限制，在常数相关性下实现精确匹配。

Method: 设计多项式时间算法，通过计算和比较一族特别选取的"宽"树的加权计数来实现匹配。算法在d ≥ polylog(n)条件下，对任何ρ² > √α（α≈0.338为Otter树计数常数）以高概率成功。

Result: 1) 给出了多项式时间算法，在常数相关性下实现精确恢复；2) 改进了信息论保证，精确恢复条件为ρ² ≳ max{log n/d, √(log n/n)}；3) 低度优势计算表明ρ² > √α是任何树计数算法的必要条件。

Conclusion: 本文首次在高维Procrustes匹配问题中实现了常数相关性下的精确恢复，建立了算法-理论的匹配边界，并通过低度优势分析揭示了树计数方法的极限。

Abstract: Suppose we observe two sets of $n$ Gaussian vectors in $\mathbb{R}^d$, with the promise that, after applying a permutation of $[n]$ and a rotation of $\mathbb{R}^d$, the two sets are $ρ$-correlated. The Procrustes matching problem asks us to recover the unknown permutation of $[n]$ that aligns the two sets. The problem is well-studied in the low-dimensional regime $d=O(\log n)$, but the high-dimensional regime $d\gg \log n$ has remained largely uncharted: prior matching guarantees require nearly perfect correlation $ρ=1-o(1)$, even for information-theoretic recovery.
  Our main result is a polynomial-time algorithm for exact recovery at constant correlation. The algorithm works by computing and comparing weighted counts of a specially chosen family of ``wide'' trees. So long as $d\ge \mathrm{polylog}(n)$, the algorithm succeeds with high probability for any $ρ^2>\sqrtα$, where $α\approx 0.338$ is Otter's tree-counting constant.
  We complement this algorithmic result with an improved information-theoretic guarantee, showing that exact recovery is possible when $ρ^2 \gtrsim \max\{\log n/d,\sqrt{\log n/n}\}$. We also carry out a low-degree advantage calculation, which suggests that the condition $ρ^2 > \sqrtα$ is necessary for any tree-counting algorithm.

</details>


### [174] [Score Accuracy Along the Forward Diffusion Does Not Certify Numerical Stability in Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08757)
*Yiwei Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了分数匹配中正向边缘误差小并不能保证反向离散采样过程的数值稳定性。尽管反向过程在路径空间总变差意义上可以任意接近真实过程，但Euler-Maruyama离散化会导致所有正阶矩发散且Wasserstein距离发散。通过投影到数据支撑的凸集上可以恢复稳定性，实验验证了该现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数匹配通过匹配正向边缘分数来训练生成模型，但反向采样时评估的是学习到的分数沿着自身轨迹的值。正向边缘误差小是否足以保证反向离散采样的数值稳定性是一个理论空白，现有研究对此缺乏深入认识。

Method: 理论构造：构建一族光滑分数场使得正向边缘L^2误差任意小，但反向过程Euler-Maruyama离散化的所有正阶矩发散且Wasserstein距离发散。同时构造有界全局Lipschitz去噪器家族，保持正向误差和路径空间总变差趋于零但W_p发散。正面结果：将去噪器投影到数据支撑的已知有界闭凸集上可恢复矩有界性和Wasserstein收敛性。实验：使用DiT风格网络验证数值轨迹的异常增长及投影抑制效果。

Result: 1) 存在光滑分数场，正向边缘L^2误差任意小，反向Euler-Maruyama在概率收敛下所有正阶矩发散，所有W_p (p≥1)发散。2) 存在有界全局Lipschitz去噪器家族，正向误差和路径空间TV趋于0但W_p发散。3) 投影到数据支撑的凸集可以保证矩有界和Wasserstein收敛。4) 实验证实了罕见轨迹上的数值发散及投影的有效抑制。

Conclusion: 正向边缘误差小不足以保证反向离散采样的数值稳定性，存在矩发散和Wasserstein距离发散的危险。投影到数据支撑的凸集是一种简单有效的修复策略。这为分数匹配生成模型的可靠部署提供了重要理论指导。

Abstract: Score matching controls average error under the forward marginals, but a discretized reverse-time sampler evaluates the learned score along its own trajectory. We show that small forward-marginal error does not guarantee numerical stability. We construct a single smooth score field with arbitrarily small forward-marginal $L^2$ error. The learned reverse-time process is nonexplosive, has moments of every order, and can be arbitrarily close to the exact reverse-time process in path-space total variation. Yet its Euler--Maruyama discretizations converge in probability while every positive moment diverges. Thus weak convergence can hold even though every Wasserstein distance $W_p$, $p\ge1$, diverges.
  The same failure can occur within one fixed finite neural architecture. We construct a family of bounded, globally Lipschitz denoisers for which both the forward-marginal error and the path-space total variation distance tend to zero, while their Euler--Maruyama endpoints diverge in every $W_p$. For compactly supported data, we also give a simple positive result. Projecting the learned denoiser onto a known bounded closed convex set containing the support preserves pointwise accuracy, gives grid-uniform moment bounds, and yields Wasserstein convergence under mild local regularity. Experiments with a small fixed DiT-style network show large growth along rare numerical trajectories and its suppression by denoiser projection, while overall trajectory errors remain small.

</details>
